We evaluate UniTexture on OpenVLA and $π_{0.5}$ across diverse manipulation tasks and multiple evaluation settings. UniTexture reduces the mean task success rate from 90.0% under benign conditions to 48.4% under attack, induces target-aligned action shifts, and further exhibits cross-suite and cross-model transfer without re-optimization. Together, these findings reveal shared cross-task vulnerabilities in multitask VLAs that can be systematically exploited through a single adversarial surface texture.

</details>


### [163] [Evaluation of Clinically Steerable Retinal Image Generation from Foundation Model Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13455)
*Zuzanna A. Wakefield-Skórniewska,Bartłomiej W. Papież*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了四种视网膜基础模型在表示分词器框架下的可控图像生成能力，发现生成的表示和图像能忠实继承表型信息（优于传统潜在扩散），但在真实图像分类器上评估时优势消失，揭示了一个未被表征的"合成到真实"表示差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学基础模型能从潜在表示中学习临床意义上的表型信息，但其潜在表示是否支持可控图像生成尚未被探索。作者希望研究基础模型的潜在空间中编码的人口统计学和临床信息在合成图像生成过程中是否得以保留，以及这些合成表示能否与传统方法匹敌。

Method: 在表示分词器框架内评估四种视网膜基础模型，生成合成图像和表示，并在多个下游预测任务上将其与传统潜在扩散方法进行比较。同时使用基础模型内部分类器和基于真实图像训练的独立分类器对生成结果进行评估，以验证表示保真度和泛化能力。

Result: 在所属基础模型内部评估时，生成的表示和图像能忠实继承表型信息，持续优于传统潜在扩散方法；但当用基于真实图像训练的分类器评估时，这些优势大幅消失，揭示了合成表示与真实图像分布之间先前未被表征的差距。

Conclusion: 基础模型的潜在空间为可控视网膜合成提供了强大基础，但需要更好地将合成表示与真实图像分布对齐，以弥合当前存在的合成到真实表示差距。

Abstract: Medical foundation models learn latent representations of clinically meaningful phenotypes, yet their ability to support controllable image generation remains largely unexplored. We evaluate four retinal foundation models within the representation tokenizer framework and examine whether demographic and clinical information encoded in latent representations from foundation models is preserved during synthetic image generation. We show that generated representations and images faithfully inherit phenotype information when evaluated within their originating foundation models, consistently outperforming conventional latent diffusion on multiple downstream prediction tasks. However, these gains largely disappear when evaluated using classifiers trained on real images, revealing a previously uncharacterised synthetic-to-real representation gap. These findings demonstrate that foundation-model latent spaces provide a powerful substrate for controllable retinal synthesis while highlighting the need to better align synthetic representations with real-image distributions.

</details>


### [164] [Fine-Grained Action Recognition with Cross-Attentive Latent Sparse Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13458)
*Imtiaz Ul Hassan,Tasweer Ahmad,Nik Bessis,Ardhendu Behera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FineX 是一种面向细粒度人类动作识别（FHAR）的多模态融合框架，将 RGB 外观、姿态热图几何与骨骼图拓扑分解为三类细粒度线索，通过成对交叉注意力实现流间信息交换，并采用流式稀疏混合专家（MoE）进行条件化特征精炼，在 Gym99、Gym288 和 Diving48 上达到最先进水平，尤其在长尾 Gym288 上将平均类别准确率提升 7.6 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度人类动作识别需要区分在身体姿态、时序或局部外观上高度相似的动作。现有的 RGB 表示保留视觉上下文但丢失关节级几何信息，而骨骼表示编码运动学但舍弃密集空间细节，两者互补却难以有效融合，缺乏统一框架同时利用视觉上下文与关节几何信息。

Method: FineX 将细粒度线索因子化为三个流：RGB 外观、姿态热图几何和骨骼图拓扑。方法包含两个核心模块：(1) 成对交叉注意力实现对称的、保持流身份的信息交换；(2) 流式潜在稀疏混合专家（Streamwise Latent Sparse Mixture-of-Experts），将每个表示路由到由内容决定的一组共享专家，并以负载均衡目标作为正则化约束。

Result: FineX 在 Gym99、Gym288 和 Diving48 三个基准上均取得最先进结果。在长尾分布的 Gym288 上，平均类别准确率从 68.6% 提升至 76.2%（+7.6 个百分点），且无需文本监督或大规模视觉-语言预训练。

Conclusion: 结构化视觉-姿态-图融合（visual-pose-graph fusion）与条件化专家精炼相结合对细粒度人类动作识别具有显著优势，证明了在无需大规模多模态预训练的前提下，显式分解互补线索并促进跨模态信息交换是提升 FHAR 性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Fine-grained human action recognition (FHAR) must distinguish visually similar actions that differ mainly in body configuration, timing, or local appearance. RGB representations retain visual context but often suppress joint-level geometry, whereas skeleton representations encode kinematics but discard dense spatial detail. We introduce FineX, which factorizes fine-grained cues into RGB appearance, pose heatmap geometry, and skeletal-graph topology. Pairwise cross-attention enables symmetric, stream-preserving information exchange, followed by a streamwise latent sparse Mixture-of-Experts that routes each representation to a content-dependent subset of shared experts, regularized by a load-balancing objective. FineX achieves state-of-the-art results on Gym99, Gym288, and Diving48. On the long-tailed Gym288, it raises mean class accuracy from 68.6% to 76.2% (+7.6 points) without textual supervision or large-scale vision-language pre-training, demonstrating the benefit of structured visual-pose-graph fusion and conditional expert refinement for FHAR.

</details>


### [165] [SNM-VFI: Symmetric Nonlinear Motion-Guided Generative Video Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13460)
*Jisoo Jeong,Hong Cai,Jamie Menjay Lin,Hanno Ackermann,Hyeonjun Sim,Yinhao Zhu,Yunxiao Shi,Fatih Porikli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SNM-VFI，一种无需训练的框架，结合预训练光流模型和视频扩散模型实现运动可控的生成式视频帧插值。通过对称非线性运动模型构造多帧光流中间帧和置信度图，将其作为潜在先验引导扩散模型生成高质量中间帧，并在遮挡等不确定区域融合光流预测与扩散生成细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于扩散的VFI方法通常从随机噪声合成中间帧，难以保持密集运动对应关系，导致运动保持能力和感知质量不佳。现有方法缺乏对运动一致性的显式引导，需要在生成过程中引入光流等运动信息来提升插值质量。

Method: 1) 使用预训练光流模型基于对称非线性运动模型构造多帧光流中间帧及置信度图；2) 将光流引导的中间帧编码为潜在先验，用于初始化和迭代引导预训练视频扩散模型；3) 利用置信度图对结构可靠的光流预测结果与扩散生成细节在遮挡和物体边界等不确定区域进行融合。

Result: 在DAVIS、Sintel和KITTI等具有挑战性的基准上，SNM-VFI实现了强感知质量、有竞争力的重建精度和跨多种运动场景的稳健时间一致性。

Conclusion: SNM-VFI有效解决了扩散式VFI中运动对应保持的难题，通过光流先验引导扩散过程，在保持运动结构的同时提升感知真实感，是一种无需训练的通用VFI框架。

Abstract: We propose Symmetric Nonlinear Motion-guided Generative Video Frame Interpolation (SNM-VFI), a training-free framework for motion-controllable generative video frame interpolation with pre-trained optical flow and video diffusion models. Unlike conventional diffusion-based VFI methods that synthesize intermediate frames from random noise, SNM-VFI guides the generative process with correspondence-aware frames produced by a symmetric nonlinear motion model. Specifically, we first utilize a pre-trained optical flow model to construct multi-frame nonlinear flow-based intermediate frames and confidence maps. These flow-guided frames are then encoded as latent priors to initialize and iteratively guide a pre-trained Video Diffusion model, enabling the diffusion model to preserve dense motion correspondence while improving perceptual realism. To further enhance output quality, we employ confidence maps to fuse structurally reliable flow-based predictions with diffusion-generated details in uncertain regions such as occlusions and object boundaries. Extensive evaluations on challenging benchmarks, including DAVIS, Sintel, and KITTI, demonstrate that SNM-VFI achieves strong perceptual quality, competitive reconstruction accuracy, and robust temporal coherence across diverse motion scenarios.

</details>


### [166] [MLLM-Routed Heterogeneous Ensembles for Robust Cross-Dataset Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13463)
*Daniel Perkins,John Squires,Janou Milligan,Chandra Raskoti,Linda Ungerboeck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ARMDIL——一种基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）代理的自适应路由器，用于多领域图像分类。该集成系统动态地将每张图像路由到最合适的视觉骨干网络（包括ResNet、自监督学习模型和视觉-语言模型），这些模型在统一标签空间上训练，涵盖多个分布和特征各异的数据集。实验表明ARMDIL能有效权衡各架构的优势与不足，与专门训练的基于路由器的方法表现相当，同时通过简单的提示修改即可集成新信息，并借助自然语言推理轨迹增强可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像分类模型通常针对单一任务特定数据集训练，在跨领域和难度级别泛化方面表现不佳。作者旨在解决这一局限性，构建能够适应多样化视觉域的通用视觉系统，为AI助手和自主机器人等更可靠的通用心智系统铺路。

Method: ARMDIL采用集成学习框架，使用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）作为智能代理，动态决定将每张图像路由到最合适的视觉骨干网络。集成中包含三类异构模型：卷积神经网络（ResNets）、自监督表征学习模型（SSL）和视觉-语言模型（VLMs），所有模型在由多个分布和特征不同的图像数据集构建的统一标签空间上进行训练。新信息可通过简单的提示词修改被集成，且路由决策伴随自然语言推理痕迹以增强可解释性。

Result: 经验评估揭示了不同架构在不同视觉领域中的独特能力和缺陷。ARMDIL能够有效导航这些权衡，在性能上与专门训练的路由器方法相当。此外，它大幅提升了适应性（通过提示词修改集成新信息）和可解释性（自然语言推理轨迹）。

Conclusion: ARMDIL通过利用MLLM代理的推理能力进行动态模型路由，在跨数据集图像分类上取得了与专用训练路由器竞争的成果，同时提供了更优的适应性和可解释性，为通用视觉系统的发展提供了有效方向。

Abstract: Modern image classification models excel when trained on single task-specific datasets but often struggle to generalize across domains and difficulty levels. We propose ARMDIL, an Adaptive Router for Multi-Domain Image classification with LLMs. ARMDIL is an ensemble that uses a multimodal large language model (MLLM) agent to dynamically route each image to the most suitable vision backbone. Our diverse ensemble employs convolutional neural networks (ResNets), self-supervised representation learners (SSL), and vision-language models (VLMs), each trained on a unified label space constructed from multiple image datasets with differing distributions and characteristics. Empirical evaluations illuminate the distinct capabilities and vulnerabilities of each architecture across disparate visual domains. Crucially, we show that ARMDIL effectively navigates these trade-offs, performing competitively with specialized training-based routers. Furthermore, it drastically improves adaptability by allowing new information to be integrated via simple prompt modifications, while enhancing interpretability through natural language reasoning traces. These advances in cross-dataset image classification pave the way for more reliable general-purpose vision systems such as AI assistants and autonomous robots.

</details>


### [167] [MapRoute++: Surrogate-Guided Semantic Routing for Visual Concept Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13478)
*Ashok Urlana,L. D. M. S. Sai Teja,Vivek Hruday Kavuri,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Genμ 2.0挑战赛任务3（视觉概念遗忘）的方法，在MapRoute框架基础上引入任务特定训练目标、更丰富的概念表示和基于语义路由的概念特定映射器选择，在Stable Diffusion v1.4上使用ERR指标平均超越SOTA基线12.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉概念遗忘（unlearning）是生成模型安全性和合规性的关键需求，需要在去除目标概念的同时保留无关概念以及语义相邻概念，现有方法在这方面的平衡表现有限。

Method: 以MapRoute为基础，引入三类改进：（1）任务特定的训练目标以增强遗忘效果；（2）更丰富的概念表示以捕获概念的多样语义；（3）语义路由机制用于概念特定的映射器（mapper）选择。

Result: 在官方基准测试中，使用Stable Diffusion v1.4上的Erasing-Retention-Robustness（ERR）指标进行评估，方法在五个概念类别上的平均性能超过了最先进的基线12.1%。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法在保持无关概念和语义相邻概念的保留能力的同时，显著提升了概念遗忘的鲁棒性和效果。

Abstract: We present our submission to Task 3 of the Gen$μ$ 2.0 Challenge on visual concept unlearning. Building on MapRoute, we introduce task-specific training objectives, richer concept representations, and semantic routing for concept-specific mapper selection. Our approach improves robust concept removal while preserving unrelated and semantically adjacent concepts. On the official benchmark, evaluated using the Erasing-Retention-Robustness (ERR) metric on Stable Diffusion v1.4, our method outperforms the state-of-the-art baseline by 12.1\% on average across the five concept categories, achieving substantial gains.

</details>


### [168] [DreamX-Phi 1.0: Action-Conditioned Video World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13489)
*DreamX Team,Rui Chen,Xiangxiang Chu,Geng Li,Jifan Li,Qingfeng Shi,Datao Tang,Jing Tang,Jun Wang,Pengfei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamX-Phi 1.0是一个动作条件化的视频世界模型，用于机器人操纵预测，通过PRoPE-style几何编码、深度分支、SAM3掩码和V-JEPA教师保持预测的真实性与一致性，并在WorldArena 2.0挑战赛中获得Track 1第一名和Track 2第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频世界模型在预测未来观测时虽然能产生逼真的画面，但预测的忠实度不足——滚动的结果可能移动了错误的机械臂或丢失了被操纵的对象，无法确保预测遵守每个机械臂的命令路径。

Method: (1) 通过PRoPE-style几何编码将每臂的SE(3)变换注入注意力机制，保持臂身份和刚体运动结构；(2) 增加轻量级深度分支以建模场景级几何；(3) 使用SAM3掩码配合冻结的V-JEPA教师模型维持抓取过程中小物体的对象一致性；(4) 通过分布匹配蒸馏将多步生成器蒸馏为少步学生模型以实现高效部署。

Result: 在WorldArena 2.0挑战赛中，DreamX-Phi 1.0在Track 1上取得第一名，在Track 2上取得第二名。

Conclusion: 通过几何编码和对象一致性保持机制的联合设计，动作条件化的视频世界模型可以同时实现预测的真实性与对物理操纵指令的忠实性，且通过蒸馏技术可高效部署。

Abstract: We present \textbf{DreamX-Phi 1.0}, an action-conditioned video world model for robotic manipulation that, given an observed frame, a language instruction, and a prescribed action sequence comprising end-effector poses and gripper states, predicts the resulting future observations. Yet realism alone does not guarantee faithfulness: a convincing rollout can still move the wrong arm or lose the manipulated object. To ensure the prediction respects each arm's commanded path, we inject per-arm $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ transformations into attention via \textbf{PRoPE-style geometric encoding}, preserving arm identity and rigid-motion structure. Action control alone does not fully constrain scene geometry or the evolution of small manipulated objects. We therefore add a lightweight \textbf{depth branch} for scene-level geometry and use \textbf{SAM3 masks} with a frozen \textbf{V-JEPA teacher} to maintain object consistency throughout grasping. We further distill the multi-step generator into a few-step student via distribution-matching distillation for efficient deployment. At the time of writing, \model{} achieves first place on Track~1 and second place on Track~2 of the WorldArena~2.0 Challenge. Our model and code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [169] [TraVEL: Trajectory-Guided Video Embedding Learning for Driving-Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13495)
*Yi-Chung Chen,Philip Jacobson,Tom Lampo,Yiren Lu,Jin Yao,David I. Inouye,Jing Gao,Danhua Guo,Burhan Yaman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TraVEL框架，利用自车轨迹相似度作为奖励信号，通过GRPO优化对通用多模态嵌入模型进行运动感知微调，显著提升驾驶视频检索中运动中心事件（如转弯、加减速）的检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从大规模驾驶日志中高效检索相关片段对数据整理、模型开发和安全分析至关重要。现有基于规则的系统需要专家定义规则和复杂感知管线，而通用多模态嵌入模型虽简单高效，却依赖静态场景上下文线索，难以区分运动中心事件（如左转与右转、加速与减速）。因此需要一种既能保持单向量嵌入高效性、又能捕捉细粒度运动信息的检索方法。

Method: 首先在nuReasoning数据集上基于配对的视频片段和推理轨迹，使用InfoNCE目标对Qwen3-VL-Embedding进行微调（SFT阶段）。随后提出TraVEL框架，利用自车轨迹相似度作为GRPO（组相对策略优化）中的奖励信号，进行运动感知的强化微调。轨迹仅在训练阶段作为特权监督信号使用，推理时仍然仅依赖单向量视频嵌入，无需自车姿态、专家规则或辅助感知输出。此外构建了基于nuReasoning的驾驶视频检索基准。

Result: 实验表明TraVEL在多个模型规模上均提升了运动中心检索性能：相比SFT，在2B规模上纵向和横向mAP分别提升9.8和4.7个百分点，在8B规模上分别提升7.2和1.5个百分点。

Conclusion: TraVEL将物理接地监督（轨迹）与高效嵌入搜索相结合，既保留了单向量嵌入的检索效率，又通过轨迹相似度奖励增强了模型对运动中心事件的区分能力，为驾驶视频检索提供了一种无需专家规则和辅助感知的实用方案。

Abstract: Efficiently retrieving relevant clips from large-scale driving logs is essential for data curation, model development, and safety analysis. Structured and rule-based retrieval systems can explicitly target driving events, but typically require expert-defined rules, auxiliary data, and multi-stage perception pipelines. Multimodal embedding models offer a simpler and more efficient alternative by representing each video with a single searchable vector. However, general-purpose models often rely on shortcuts from static scene context and struggle to distinguish motion-centric events, such as turning left versus right or accelerating versus decelerating. In this work, we study how to adapt a general-purpose multimodal embedding model to driving-video retrieval. We first fine-tune Qwen3-VL-Embedding on paired clips and reasoning traces from nuReasoning using an InfoNCE objective. While this stage substantially improves overall retrieval, caption supervision alone remains insufficient for fine-grained motion understanding. We therefore introduce TraVEL (Trajectory-Guided Video Embedding Learning), a motion-aware fine-tuning framework that uses ego-trajectory similarity as a reward within Group Relative Policy Optimization. Trajectories serve only as privileged training supervision; retrieval still operates on single-vector video embeddings without ego poses, expert rules, or auxiliary perception outputs. We further construct a driving-video retrieval benchmark from nuReasoning. Experiments show that TraVEL improves motion-centric retrieval across model scales: relative to SFT, it raises longitudinal and lateral mAP by 9.8 and 4.7 points at 2B, with corresponding gains of 7.2 and 1.5 points at 8B. TraVEL thus combines physically grounded supervision with efficient embedding-based search.

</details>


### [170] [GS$^{2}$CI: Robust Gaussian Splatting For Snapshot Compressive Imaging via Large Vision Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13502)
*Yanming Yang,Chenxi Song,Ping Wang,Xin Yuan,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从单次快照压缩成像（SCI）测量中重建高质量3D场景的框架，结合3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）与大规模视觉基础模型（VFM）先验，通过SCI感知的高斯优化和伪视图监督实现粗到细的重建，并引入针对SCI特性的不透明度引导的密度化策略（OSGR），在多个基准上取得最优综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快照压缩成像（SCI）可在单次2D测量中压缩时间或空间信息，实现高速视频采集和多视角场景捕捉。尽管已有研究探索SCI用于3D场景重建，但现有方法面临信息丢失、视点多样性有限以及联合优化3D表示与相机位姿的计算负担等重大挑战。因此，需要一种能高效从单次SCI测量重建高质量3D场景的新方法。

Method: 本方法主要包含三个部分：(1) 主重建阶段：利用测量衍生的3D视觉基础模型（VFM）初始化与SCI感知的高斯优化相结合，重建3D场景；(2) 辅助细化阶段：在粗阶段收敛后，使用2D VFM在合成视点提供伪视图监督，用于局部外观细化；(3) OSGR密度化策略：引入不透明度引导的拆分与增长调节（OSGR），通过局部不透明度统计增强拆分候选，通过均值不透明度调节抑制损失补偿性的不透明度膨胀，并用显式的候选比例和高斯数量约束限制表示增长，以解决SCI监督模糊性导致的3DGS优化不稳定性。

Result: 在多个基准上的广泛实验表明，该方法取得了最强的综合性能，在重建质量和视点变化鲁棒性方面均处于领先水平，同时保持了有竞争力的计算效率。

Conclusion: 本文验证了结合3DGS与视觉基础模型先验能够有效克服单次SCI测量中信息丢失和视点多样性有限的挑战，实现高质量3D场景重建。所提出的OSGR策略有效提升了SCI监督下的3DGS优化稳定性，为SCI驱动的3D重建提供了新的可行范式。

Abstract: Snapshot Compressive Imaging (SCI) offers an efficient solution for high-speed video acquisition and, under exposure-time camera--scene relative motion, multi-view scene capture by compressing temporal or spatial information into a single 2D measurement. While recent studies have explored SCI for 3D scene reconstruction, existing methods struggle with significant challenges due to information loss, limited viewpoint diversity, and the computational burden of jointly optimizing 3D representations and camera poses. In this work, we propose a novel framework that reconstructs high-quality 3D scenes from a single SCI measurement by leveraging 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and the powerful priors of large-scale vision foundation models (VFMs). Our primary reconstruction combines measurement-derived 3D VFM initialization with SCI-aware Gaussian optimization. After coarse-stage convergence, an auxiliary 2D VFM provides pseudo-view supervision at synthesized viewpoints for local appearance refinement. To further address the instability caused by ambiguous SCI supervision during 3DGS optimization, we introduce Opacity-Guided Splitting and Growth Regulation (OSGR), an SCI-specific densification strategy that augments split candidates using local opacity statistics, discourages loss-compensating opacity inflation through mean-opacity regulation, and bounds representation growth with explicit candidate-ratio and Gaussian-count constraints. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves the strongest overall performance, combining leading reconstruction quality and robustness to viewpoint variation with competitive computational efficiency.

</details>


### [171] [TabSOM: A tabular-to-image encoding method based on self-organizing maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13513)
*David Chushig-Muzo,María Ángeles Rodríguez de Cara,Eva Milara,Francisco J. Lara-Abelenda,Luis Zhinin-Vera,Diego H. Peluffo-Ordóñez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TabSOM是一种基于自组织映射(SOM)的表格数据到图像编码方法，通过固定画布位置和特征关系图将表格数据转换为图像表示，在十二种现有方法对比中表现最优，同时提供两种可解释性工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格到图像的转换方法（如基于t-SNE、UMAP、PCA的特征映射）仅编码每个特征的边缘值，丢弃了特征之间的关系信息，导致信息丢失。作者旨在提出一种既能利用CNN和Vision Transformer的高预测性能、又能保留特征间关系信息且具备可解释性的映射方法。

Method: TabSOM基于自组织映射构建：通过匈牙利算法将每个特征分配到组件平面中的固定画布位置，实现无碰撞布局；利用SOM组件平面提取特征间的成对关系图。生成的图像堆叠两个多尺度节点通道——一个以固定尺度编码特征值，另一个以特征间的空间连接编码成对交互。此外提出两种可解释性方法：基于原型启发的部分依赖图和类分离重要性评分。

Result: 在十二种现有表格到图像方法及公开二分类数据集的基准测试中，TabSOM在每个数据集上排名第一或第二，且在所有评估方法中方差最低。类分离重要性评分与Random Forest、XGBoost和SHAP在顶级特征上表现合理一致，同时捕获输入数据中互补的结构信息。

Conclusion: TabSOM提供了将深度学习架构应用于表格数据的有效且可解释的方案，弥合了该领域中性能与可解释性之间的差距，证明了在保持可解释性的同时实现高性能的可行性。

Abstract: Tabular-to-image methods have emerged as novel approaches to leverage the high predictive performance of convolutional neural networks and vision transformers. They convert tabular data into image representations, mapping each feature at a fixed pixel location derived from a dimensionality-reduction method (e.g., t-SNE, UMAP, PCA). However, they encode only the marginal value of each feature and discard information about feature relationships. We propose TabSOM, a tabular-to-image encoding built on the Self-Organizing Map (SOM), which provides: (i) a spatial layout in which every input feature occupies a fixed canvas position derived from its component plane via collision-free Hungarian assignment; and (ii) a graph that captures pairwise feature relationships derived from the SOM component planes. The resulting image stacks two multi-scale node channels: one encodes feature values at fixed scales, while the other encodes pairwise feature interactions as spatial connections between related features. Two SOM-derived interpretability approaches are introduced: a prototype-inspired partial dependence plot and a class--separation importance score. Benchmarked against twelve existing tabular-to-image methods across public binary-classification datasets, TabSOM ranks first or second on every dataset and achieves the lowest variance of any method evaluated. Interpretability obtained with TabSOM was validated against Random Forest, XGBoost, and SHAP, the class-separation score shows reasonable agreement with established baselines on the top-ranked features while capturing complementary structural information from input data. These results demonstrate that TabSOM provides an effective and interpretable approach for applying deep learning architectures to tabular data, bridging the performance--interpretability gap in this domain.

</details>


### [172] [SCULPT: Subtractive Composition for 3D Part Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13541)
*Sikuang Li,Chen Yang,Jiemin Fang,Jiazhong Cen,Yuhe Wei,Jichen Pang,Wei Shen,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SCULPT框架，通过减法合成的方式实现部分感知的3D生成。在结构化3D潜空间中，迭代应用联合分割预测器，同时生成一个提取的部分和剩余物体，通过耦合去噪过程确保"提取部分"与"更新后的剩余部分"共同生成而非事后拼接。联合分割预测器在两个输出的稀疏3D支持并集上处理，允许相邻支持重叠而非强制不相交的体素划分。滚动过程在剩余部分支持为空或达到安全上限时结束，使生成部分的数量能在界内自适应。实验在PartObjaverse上取得SOTA几何效果，同时保持组装后的完整物体重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部分感知3D生成方法存在两类局限：分割类方法在完整物体生成后才确定部分边界，虽保留生成几何但后置固定物体；加法类方法从预定义布局、框或token合成部分后再整合，暴露部分基数但共享边界容易出现间隙、互穿和材质不连续问题。本文旨在通过减法合成策略克服上述两类方法的固有缺陷。

Method: SCULPT框架采用减法合成策略：1) 在结构化3D潜空间中表示完整物体；2) 迭代应用联合分割预测器（joint split predictor），每步同时生成一个提取的部分和一个更新后的剩余物体；3) 预测器执行耦合去噪过程，条件化于图像和当前3D状态，确保提取部分与剩余部分一起生成；4) 处理两个输出时使用其稀疏3D支持并集，允许相邻支持重叠而非强制不相交体素划分；5) 滚动终止条件为剩余支持为空或达到安全上限，实现部分数量的自适应生成。

Result: 在PartObjaverse基准上取得SOTA几何效果，同时在部分组装后保持强完整的物体重建质量。在四个数据集图像、一个文本到图像生成输入和一个真实世界照片上的结果表明，该方法能实现超越基准的细粒度纹理部分分解。

Conclusion: SCULPT通过减法合成范式有效解决了现有部分感知3D生成中"先整体后分割"与"先部件后整合"的矛盾，实现了生成几何质量与部分结构可编辑性的统一，并在多样化的输入来源上展示了细粒度的纹理部分分解能力。

Abstract: Part-aware 3D generation aims to create digital assets that are coherent as complete objects while exposing structural parts for editing, material assignment, animation, and reuse. Existing methods impose this structure outside the native generation loop: segmentation-based methods partition an already generated shape, while additive methods synthesize parts from predefined layouts, boxes, or tokens and then reconcile them into a whole. The former preserves the generated geometry but fixes the object before part boundaries are determined; the latter exposes part cardinality but often leaves shared boundaries vulnerable to gaps, interpenetrations, and material discontinuities. In this paper, we propose SCULPT, a framework that addresses these challenges through subtractive composition. Given a complete object represented in a structured 3D latent space, SCULPT iteratively applies a joint split predictor to generate one extracted part together with the remaining object. The predictor performs a coupled denoising process conditioned on both the image and the current 3D state, so the extracted part and updated remainder are generated together rather than reconciled after generation. The joint split predictor processes both outputs on the union of their native sparse 3D supports, allowing neighboring supports to overlap rather than imposing a disjoint voxel partition. The rollout ends when the remainder support becomes empty or reaches a fixed safety cap, allowing the number of generated parts to adapt to each object within that bound. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art geometry on PartObjaverse while preserving strong complete-object reconstruction after part assembly. Results on four dataset images, one text-to-image-generated input, and one real-world photograph further show fine-grained textured part decomposition beyond the benchmark.

</details>


### [173] [Alaya-EVOKE: From Linear-Scaling Supervision to Endless World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13546)
*Yuanyang Yin,Gongxuan Wang,Yifan Zhan,Chuanhao Li,Kaipeng Zhang,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Evoke通过外部化持久世界状态（camera-indexed世界状态银行）和重新设计教师模型实现长时程监督，解决了交互式世界模型中会话长度与记忆保持的权衡以及少步生成能力受限于教师的问题，在384×640分辨率下每个1.5秒块仅需2.11秒生成，成为WBench上最优的三步世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型需要同时满足持久记忆、响应式交互和长时程生成三个要求，但这些要求对模型构成冲突：在denoiser上下文或KV缓存中维护历史会导致成本不断增长，迫使在会话长度和保留记忆之间做出权衡；同时，低延迟交互依赖少步生成，而少步生成的能力受其教师模型的上限约束。这两大局限性亟待解决。

Method: Evoke采用双管齐下的方法：(1)将场景几何外部化到camera-indexed的世界状态银行中，仅检索与当前视角相关的信息，使denoiser上下文随会话增长保持有界；(2)重新设计教师模型以支持长时程监督：其稀疏注意力结合了分块分组（chunk-wise grouping）、选定远处帧的检索以及线性注意力全局状态，使内存和计算呈线性增长；通过30秒的分布匹配目标（在自强制rollout下应用），将长时程能力和响应式条件控制蒸馏到无需分类器引导的三步学生模型中。

Result: 在单张H200上、384×640分辨率下，每个1.5秒块生成耗时2.11秒。作为三步世界模型，Evoke在WBench上达到最先进性能，同时在VBench-Long和VBench-2.0上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: Evoke通过有界上下文和循环外部记忆，实现了开放式的持续演化生成，有效抵抗长期内容漂移（这在短窗口内局部看似合理），同时支持分块条件控制实现序列中的提示变更和事件控制，解决了交互式世界模型在长时程、记忆和交互性之间的核心矛盾。

Abstract: Interactive world models must support persistent memory, responsive interaction, and long-horizon generation, yet these requirements place conflicting demands on the model. Maintaining history in the denoiser context or key-value cache incurs growing cost, forcing a trade-off between session length and retained memory, while low-latency interaction relies on few-step generation whose capabilities are bounded by its teacher. Evoke addresses both limitations by externalizing persistent world state and redesigning the teacher for long-horizon interactive generation. Scene geometry is maintained in an external, camera-indexed world state bank, from which only view-relevant information is retrieved, keeping the denoiser context bounded as the session grows. Rather than treating the teacher as a fixed generator, we design it for long-horizon supervision: its sparse attention combines chunk-wise grouping, retrieval of selected distant frames, and a linear-attention global state, yielding linear growth in memory and compute while enabling supervision over long horizons. Such supervision exposes content drift that stays locally plausible within short windows, while per-chunk conditioning enables prompt changes and event control throughout the sequence. A 30-second distribution-matching objective, applied under self-forced rollouts, transfers both capabilities to a three-step student that uses no classifier-free guidance, improving resistance to long-term drift while preserving responsive conditioning. With bounded context and recurrent external memory, Evoke supports open-ended, continuously evolving generation; on a single H200 at $384\times 640$, each $1.5\,\mathrm{s}$ chunk is generated in $2.11\,\mathrm{s}$. As a three-step world model, Evoke achieves state-of-the-art performance on WBench while remaining competitive on VBench-Long and VBench-2.0.

</details>


### [174] [PlayWorld: Benchmarking World Models with Agent Players over Long-Horizon Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13552)
*Kaixin Ding,Xi Chen,Minghong Cai,Zhiyuan Xu,Yiyang Wang,Yuxiang Lu,Junyi Li,Shuyang Chen,Yuan Gao,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PlayWorld基准，通过多模态Agent Player与视频世界模型交互以完成指定长程目标，从而公平评估不同模型的交互能力。基准包含171个场景，从几何一致性、交互保真度、视外景演化、洞察演化四个核心维度及视频质量与可控性基础维度评估9个SOTA模型，发现现有模型在长程交互目标上仍不可靠，尤其在空间一致性和持久状态演化方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型近期在长序列一致性和动作可控性上表现优异，但公平比较这些交互式模型仍然困难。固定动作条件下的评估不适合跨模型比较，因为实现同一目标所需的动作序列在不同模型间差异很大，需要一种以目标为导向的交互式评估范式。

Method: 采用多模态Agent Player与世界模型交互以完成指定的长程目标；构建包含171个场景的PlayWorld基准，每个场景指定一个目标；沿四个核心维度（几何一致性、交互保真度、视外景演化、洞察演化）评估模型，并结合视频质量和可控性的基础能力指标。

Result: 对9个SOTA世界模型的实验表明，现有模型在长程交互目标上仍不可靠，尤其在保持空间一致性和持续状态演化方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: 当前视频世界模型在长程交互式目标上仍存在明显不足，PlayWorld提供的目标导向交互评估范式可为未来模型改进提供有效指导。

Abstract: Video world models simulate future states conditioned on current observations and user actions. Recent systems have demonstrated impressive video consistency and action controllability over long sequences. However, fairly comparing these interactive models remains challenging. In practice, a human player typically evaluates a world model by pursuing long-horizon objectives through interaction. For example, a user may turn around 360 degrees to see whether the environment remains consistent, or walk into the water and inspect whether realistic water ripples are generated. The action sequence required to achieve the same objective may vary substantially between models, making fixed action-conditioned evaluation unsuitable for cross-model comparison. To address this, we employ multi-modal Agent Players to interact with world models toward specified long-horizon objectives. Building on this paradigm, we introduce PlayWorld, a benchmark providing 171 scenarios, each with a specified objective. To evaluate performance thoroughly, we assess models along four core dimensions: geometry consistency, interaction fidelity, out-of-sight evolution, and insight evolution. In addition, we incorporate basic ability metrics for video quality and controllability. Experiments across nine state-of-the-art world models reveal that current models remain unreliable on long-horizon interactive objectives, particularly in maintaining spatial consistency and persistent state evolution. Code and data are available at https://github.com/kxding/PlayWorld.

</details>


### [175] [V-RAE: Rethinking Video Latent Spaces for Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13556)
*Minghui Guo,Shengqiong Wu,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出V-RAE视频表示自编码器，在冻结的视觉基础模型表示之上构建紧凑的生成式隐空间，通过轻量级时序池化模块去除时间冗余并保持语义结构。在K600上达到2.13 rFVD，超越所有评测的大规模预训练视频VAE，隐空间保留更多语义信息，在UCF101和K600上分别取得117.86和19.16的gFVD，收敛速度提升最高6倍，并引入tFVD时序一致性诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频自编码器的隐空间主要针对像素级重构进行优化，缺乏高层次语义组织，而重构最优的隐空间未必适合生成建模。作者希望利用冻结的视觉基础模型表征来构建既紧凑又富含语义信息的生成式视频隐空间。

Method: V-RAE在冻结的视觉基础模型表征之上构建紧凑生成隐空间：轻量级时序池化模块去除时间冗余同时保持语义结构，视频解码器从压缩特征中重建连续运动。评测了四种代表性冻结编码器用于视频重构、语义探测和类条件生成。

Result: V-RAE在K600上rFVD达到2.13，超越所有评测的大规模预训练视频VAE；隐空间比传统视频tokenizer保留更多语义信息；在匹配生成设置下，最佳变体在UCF101和K600上gFVD分别为117.86和19.16，收敛速度提升最多6倍；在Cityscapes未来视频预测上优于Wan 2.2 VAE隐空间；提出tFVD诊断指标，与下游生成质量相关性更可靠。

Conclusion: 实验表明冻结的语义表征能够有效支撑视频重构、生成和预测建模；重构质量本身不足以衡量生成效用，tFVD可作为更可靠的生成质量诊断指标。

Abstract: Latent video generation relies on autoencoders to define a compact space in which generative models operate. Although video autoencoder architectures have evolved substantially, their latent spaces are still optimized primarily for pixel-level reconstruction and provide limited high-level semantic organization. A reconstruction-optimal latent space, however, need not be well suited to generative modeling. We propose V-RAE, a video representation autoencoder that builds compact generative latents on top of frozen vision foundation model representations. A lightweight temporal pooling module removes temporal redundancy while preserving semantic structure, and a video decoder reconstructs continuous motion from the compressed features. We evaluate V-RAE with four representative frozen encoders on video reconstruction, semantic probing, and class-conditional generation. V-RAE achieves 2.13 rFVD on K600, outperforming all evaluated large-scale pretrained video VAEs. Its latents retain substantially more semantic information than conventional video tokenizer latents. Under matched generation settings, our best variant achieves gFVD scores of 117.86 and 19.16 on UCF101 and K600, respectively, while converging up to 6x faster}. We further show that reconstruction quality alone is insufficient to characterize generative utility and introduce tFVD, a temporal-coherence diagnostic that correlates more reliably with downstream generation quality. Beyond video generation, V-RAE also improves future video prediction on Cityscapes over the Wan 2.2 VAE latent space under matched prediction settings. Taken together, the experiments show that frozen semantic representations can support video reconstruction, generation, and predictive modeling. The project page: https://v-rae.github.io/.

</details>


### [176] [AutoDesign: Meta-Harness Optimization for Long-Horizon Agentic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13560)
*Yaxin Luo,Haobin Jiang,Jialv Zou,Xu Huang,Wenhao Yan,Haodong Li,Zhengrong Yue,Jing Li,Xiaofu Chen,Xiaohan Zhao,Jiacheng Liu,Jiacheng Cui,Zhiqiang Shen,Xiaotong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoDesign框架，将多模态源到结构化媒体输出的转换视为长视野智能体流程，通过元harness优化器指导代码智能体基于rollout反馈递归改进harness系统。在学术论文到海报生成任务上，AutoDesign在PosterBench基准上超越商业系统Claude Design 7.45分，且集成学习到的DesignHarness可在七种配置下稳定提升性能。




<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有harness系统是静态的，无法与人类设计先验对齐，也无法通过经验积累实现递归自我改进，缺乏长视野智能体流程中可复用经验驱动的自优化能力。

Method: 提出AutoDesign框架，包含元harness优化器（meta-harness optimizer）和代码智能体，通过rollout反馈递归改进harness。同时构建PosterBench基准（100篇论文主数据集+10篇论文子集PosterBench-mini）用于评估。

Result: 在PosterBench Main Track上AutoDesign得分78.32，超越Claude Design 7.45分；在七种受控代码智能体模型配置下，集成DesignHarness将平均分数从54.99提升至67.39（+12.4%）；完全自主长视野循环中40分钟内执行253次工具调用和11次编辑，成本低于3美元；系统盲人类研究显示AutoDesign获得最高人类偏好。

Conclusion: AutoDesign验证了通过元harness优化器驱动代码智能体递归自我改进的范式有效性，证明基于经验积累的harness系统能够在实际多模态生成任务中显著超越静态系统，实现低成本高效率的学术海报自动生成。

Abstract: Transforming multimodal sources into condensed and structured media outputs can be fundamentally conceptualized as a long-horizon agentic process centered on a model-harness system. While an ideal harness system should align with human design priors and accumulate reusable experience through empirical exploration to drive recursive self-improvement, existing paradigms remain static and fall short of this capability. In this paper, we present AutoDesign, a framework that aligns with human design priors, where a meta-harness optimizer guides a code agent to recursively improve harness based on rollout feedback. To instantiate and evaluate this framework, we focus on the academic paper-to-poster generation task and introduce PosterBench, comprising a 100-paper Main Track spanning five disciplines and PosterBench-mini, a shared 10-paper subset for controlled evaluation. On the PosterBench Main Track, AutoDesign achieves the highest score of 78.32, surpassing the closed-source commercial system Claude Design by 7.45 points. Across seven controlled code-agent-model configurations, integrating the learned DesignHarness consistently improves performance, increasing the average PosterBench Score from 54.99 to 67.39 (+12.4%). In a fully autonomous long-horizon loop, it executes 253 tool calls and 11 editing turns within 40 minutes for under $3, reaching average conference-poster quality in human evaluation. A system-blind human study further demonstrates that AutoDesign achieves the highest human preference among evaluated systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [DCBA: Detection of Collaborative Black-Hole Attacks in Connected Dominated Set using Baiting Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12347)
*Lata B T,Venugopal K R*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于AODV路由协议的轻量级协作黑洞攻击检测与隔离技术（LW-CBH），通过定时器驱动的诱饵过程和反向追踪机制，利用MAC层控制状态消息（R-SEQ和C-SEQ）识别并隔离恶意节点，在NS-2.35中仿真验证了吞吐量、包投递率、端到端时延和归一化路由开销等性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MANET（移动自组织网络）由于缺乏中心管理且节点动态分布，极易遭受各类攻击。其中黑洞攻击通过虚假广播最短路径来破坏网络通信，协作黑洞攻击更是多个恶意节点协同攻击，严重降低网络可靠性。现有AODV路由协议在动态拓扑变化中无法有效检测恶意节点，亟需一种轻量级、高效的检测与隔离机制。

Method: 提出LW-CBH（Lightweight Collaborative Black-Hole detection）方案：在现有AODV路由协议基础上增强安全机制。具体包括：(1)基于定时器的诱饵（baiting）过程，主动引诱恶意节点暴露；(2)反向追踪（reverse tracing）设置，定位恶意节点位置；(3)利用MAC层的控制状态消息（R-SEQ和C-SEQ）以及节点连通支配集（Connected Dominated Set）来实现恶意节点的检测与隔离。

Result: 使用离散事件仿真器NS-2.35对提出的LW-CBH方案进行仿真验证，评估指标包括吞吐量（throughput）、包投递率（packet delivery ratio）、平均端到端时延（average end-to-end delay）和归一化路由开销（normalized routing overhead），结果表明该方案能有效检测并隔离协作黑洞攻击节点。

Conclusion: LW-CBH方案通过增强AODV协议，结合诱饵机制与反向追踪策略，在动态拓扑的MANET环境中能够有效检测和隔离协作黑洞攻击节点，提升网络整体性能与可靠性，弥补了传统AODV协议在检测恶意节点方面的不足。

Abstract: Mobile Ad-hoc Network (MANET) is temporary and dynamic network topology, wherein nodes are mobile in nature and distributed randomly in a network area. In MANET, nodes cooperate with each other to operate and forward data through multihop communication between source and destination. MANET is exposed to different types of attacks due to absence of central administration. However, some nodes decline to cooperate, misbehaves and appears to be malicious affecting network functionality and connectivity. Providing security and identifying malicious node has become one of the challenging research topics in MANET. Black-hole attack is considered to be most popular attack that degrades the overall network performance. Black-hole node falsely advertises the shortest path to destination intentionally to disrupt the network communication resulting in packet drop. In collaborative black-hole attacks, multiple black-hole nodes cooperate and launch attacks in order to degrade network reliability. In this article we propose a Lightweight technique to detect and isolate Collaborative Black-Hole attacks (LW-CBH) by enhancing existing AODV routing protocol. In this scheme a timer based baiting process and reverse tracing setup is used to detect malicious node through control status message in MAC layer which are Reply Sequence (R-SEQ) and Code Sequence (C-SEQ) message of connected dominated set of nodes. However existing AODV routing protocol fails to detect malicious node during dynamic topology changing in MANET. Simulation of proposed technique is performed using discrete event simulator tool NS-2.35. The simulation results are evaluated for throughput, packet delivery ratio, average end-to-end delay and normalized routing overhead.

</details>


### [178] [The energetic cost of mitigating AI attacks in cellular networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12431)
*Adrián Losada,Hao Qiang Luo-Chen,David Segura,Carlos S. Alvarez-Merino,Milan Groshev,Emil J. Khatib,Raquel Barco*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了蜂窝网络中AI/ML集成的安全漏洞与防御策略的能耗代价，指出了ML准确性、鲁棒性和能源效率三者之间的权衡挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI/ML在蜂窝网络（如O-RAN的RIC）中的广泛应用，数据驱动的算法暴露了新的安全漏洞。虽然已有防御策略有效，但其计算负担带来的能源成本在当前的能源意识背景下往往被忽视，因此需要对防御技术的能耗进行量化分析。

Method: 对一种防御技术的能耗消耗进行特征化分析，并系统阐述ML准确性、鲁棒性和能源效率三者之间形成的权衡挑战。

Result: 完成了对防御技术能耗的量化特征描述，并明确了ML准确性、鲁棒性与能源效率三元组所面临的挑战。

Conclusion: 在追求ML模型鲁棒性的同时必须考虑由此产生的能源开销，研究者在设计防御机制时需要在准确性、安全性和能源效率之间寻找平衡。

Abstract: The integration of Artificial Intelligence (AI), generally as Machine Learning (ML) algorithms, in all levels and aspects of cellular networks demonstrates the success of data-driven algorithms; for example, the Radio Intelligence Controller (RIC) of the O-RAN paradigm bestows the network with optimised radio resource allocation, load balancing or energy efficiency functions, among others. Nevertheless, this dependency on data opens new security vulnerabilities, as attackers can alter data properties and steer ML models to underperform or degrade. Conversely, the developed mitigation strategies are effective, but they generate a computational load which, in consequence, results in an energy cost generally overlooked, even in the current energy-awareness context. In this work, consumption of a defence technique is characterised, and the challenges raised by the triad of ML accuracy, robustness and energy efficiency are outlined.

</details>


### [179] [Non-Degenerate Risk Certification for Automated Security Decisions: A Decision-Contract Theory with ATT\&CK-Aligned Triage as a Worked Instance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12444)
*Zhenpeng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个结构性缺陷：任何无条件的风险界都可以通过"永不行动"的选择器（完全放弃自动化）被平凡满足，从而失去意义。作者提出了一套"决策契约"理论（decision-contract theory），包括误差守恒定律、无标签的单一容量认证、以及非退化可行动性认证，用于排除全弃权解。论文在基于LLM的入侵检测告警分流（ATT&CK对齐）场景中实证验证了该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现现有的风险认证机制存在"空洞性失败"（vacuity failure）：没有任何条件约束的风险界总是能被一个从不采取行动的选择器满足，因为它将风险驱动为零。这意味着任何风险证书如果不对"决策契约"（系统作用的输入+输出判正确的语义关系）进行限定，就会隐藏基分类器的误差。作者由此出发，希望建立一套能够结构性排除平凡解、真正衡量自动化决策能力上限的理论框架。

Method: 作者提出了一套决策契约理论：(1) 误差守恒定律：误差只在有害自动化、人类延迟（deferral）和语义掩蔽之间重新分配，总量不灭；(2) 无标签的单一容量认证：无需标注即可证明结构性的能力不足，并附带一个风险可行的精化，用于区分可恢复的阈值错位与受风险约束的不可行性；(3) 非退化可行动性证书：通过构造排除了"全弃权"解。最后在3个IDS数据集、6个LLM、4个误差率阈值上对ATT&CK对齐的告警分流任务进行实证。

Result: 在90.3%的配置中，经验性的错误归属风险保持在目标水平或以下，平均正确自动化率为83.4%。容量诊断能解释所有低效用配置；其精化能区分真正的错位与受风险约束的不可行性，并通过展示的备选阈值得到确认。训练稳定性重跑未发现任何已确认的结构性不可行性实例。使用真实细粒度攻击子类型标签验证了在真实多对一映射下的粗化-迁移恒等式，掩蔽质量虽小但非零。

Conclusion: 无条件风险认证在结构上是空泛的，除非显式定义决策契约。作者的理论框架不仅揭示了误差守恒的本质规律，还提供了无标签容量的诊断工具和非退化的可行动性认证方式，从而在理论上排除了"全弃权"这种平凡但仍然满足风险界的手段，为自动化决策系统的可靠认证提供了更严谨的基础。

Abstract: An unconditional risk bound on automated decisions can be satisfied without automating anything, since a selector that never acts drives the bound to zero. We show this is structural: any risk certificate is defined over a decision contract, the inputs a system acts on plus the semantic relation under which an output counts correct, and weakening either hides base-classifier error. We develop a decision-contract theory: an error-conservation law showing error is only reassigned among harmful automation, human deferral, and semantic masking; a label-free singleton capacity certifying structural incapacity, with a risk-feasible refinement separating recoverable threshold misalignment from risk-constrained incapacity; and a non-degenerate actionability certificate excluding all-abstain solutions by construction. We instantiate this on ATT\&CK-aligned alert triage for LLM-based intrusion detection, the setting that exposed the vacuity failure. Across 3 IDS datasets, 6 LLMs, and 4 error-rate thresholds, empirical false-attribution risk stays at or below target in 90.3% of configurations, with 83.4% mean correct automation. The capacity diagnostic explains every low-utility configuration; its refinement separates genuine misalignment from risk-constrained incapacity, confirmed by an exhibited alternative threshold; a training-stability re-run finds no confirmed structural-incapacity instance; and real fine-grained attack-subtype labels confirm the coarsening-transfer identity under a genuine many-to-one map, with small but non-zero masking mass.

</details>


### [180] [SoK: From Generation to Consumption of Privacy Documents in Software Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12511)
*Shidong Pan,Clark LaChance,Zhen Tao,Sepideh Ghanavati*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文从软件工程视角对隐私文档（如隐私政策、隐私标签等）进行了系统性综述（SoK），分析了2010至2025年间发表的290篇论文，围绕五个研究问题组织内容，识别出15个关键研究趋势、21个开放机遇，并提出四个未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私文档研究在过去数十年间显著扩展，涵盖传统隐私政策、短通知和界面级透明度机制等多种类型。随着该研究领域持续增长，研究者难以获得关于隐私文档在其生命周期中如何被创建、分析、评估和维护的连贯视图，因此需要一个统一的系统性综述来整合相关知识。

Method: 本文采用系统化文献综述（SoK）方法，从软件工程视角对2010至2025年间发表的290篇论文进行系统回顾与分析，围绕五个研究问题组织文献：(1) 隐私文档的定义与范围；(2) 生成；(3) 分析与提取；(4) 不一致性与不合规性检查；(5) 可用性评估与改进。

Result: 基于对290篇论文的系统分析，本文识别出15个关键研究趋势和21个开放机遇，并进一步勾勒出四个更广泛的研究方向：AI中心平台的新兴挑战、多样化且更新的数据基础需求、基于LLM的统一策略-代码分析、以及面向终端用户和开发者的双重可用性。

Conclusion: 本文通过提供面向生命周期的统一视角，为未来关于隐私政策和隐私文档的研究构建了共享基础，希望促进该领域的系统性和协作性发展。

Abstract: Privacy documents (e.g., privacy policies) are a central mechanism through which digital services disclose data practices and seek user consent. Over the past decades, research on privacy documents has expanded significantly, encompassing not only traditional privacy policies but also short notices (e.g., privacy labels) and interface-level transparency mechanisms. As this research area continues to grow, it has become increasingly difficult to obtain a coherent view of how privacy documents are created, analyzed, evaluated, and maintained across their lifecycle. This SoK provides a unified, lifecycle-oriented view of privacy documents from a software engineering perspective. We systematically review and analyze 290 papers published between 2010 and 2025, organizing them around five research questions that examine how privacy documents are (1) defined and scoped, (2) generated, (3) analyzed and extracted, (4) checked for inconsistencies and noncompliance, and (5) evaluated and improved for usability. Building on our findings, we identify 15 key research trends and 21 open opportunities. We further chart four broader research directions that highlight (i) emerging challenges in AI-centric platforms, (ii) the need for diverse and up-to-date data foundations, (iii) LLM-based unified policy-code analysis, and (iv) dual usability for end-users and developers. We hope this SoK provides a shared foundation for future research on privacy policies and privacy documents.

</details>


### [181] [Tracing Provenance and Detecting Tampering with Complementary LLM Watermarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12713)
*Xiaoyan Feng,Yanjun Zhang,He Zhang,Leo Yu Zhang,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种同时提供来源追踪和篡改证据的LLM文本水印方法，通过在同一token中共同嵌入鲁棒信号和脆弱信号，在检测时支持三种决策：完整、篡改、无水印。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印在编辑下保持来源追踪的鲁棒性，但这一特性也允许攻击者在保留归因的同时篡改关键内容，形成piggyback spoofing漏洞。需要一种既能保护来源又能检测篡改的水印方案。

Method: 在生成的每个token中共同嵌入鲁棒信号和脆弱信号。两个信号使用相同机制但采用独立密钥和不同的文本归一化播种窗口，使一个信号对编辑鲁棒、另一个对读者可见变化敏感。通过多轮无偏锦标赛重加权保持期望生成分布，并使用周期轮次分配模式控制两信号间的权衡。检测时，两信号的分数构成二维空间，支持完整、篡改、无水印三种决策。

Result: 在两个大语言模型和两个提示数据集上，该方法在评估的方法中取得了最高的篡改检测率，同时保持了有竞争力的归因鲁棒性和困惑度。消融研究表明，可靠的三种状态检测需要明确定义的完整性概念、两信号的共同嵌入以及互补的编辑敏感性。

Conclusion: 通过共同嵌入鲁棒和脆弱两种水印信号，可以有效解决LLM水印中来源追踪与篡改检测之间的冲突，为文本溯源提供了更全面的安全保障。

Abstract: Watermarking LLM-generated text is an important task for tracing its provenance. Existing LLM watermarks preserve provenance under editing, but this same robustness allows an adversary to alter critical content while retaining attribution, a vulnerability known as piggyback spoofing. We introduce an innovative watermark that jointly provides provenance and tamper evidence. It co-embeds a robust signal and a fragile signal into each generated token. The signals share the same mechanism but use independent keys and different seeding windows over normalized text, making one resilient to edits and the other sensitive to reader-visible changes. Multiple rounds of unbiased tournament reweighting preserve the expected generation distribution, while a periodic round-allocation pattern controls the trade-off between the two signals. At detection, their scores form a two-dimensional space supporting three decisions: Intact, Tampered, and No-Watermark. Across two large language models and two prompt datasets, our method demonstrates the highest tamper-detection rate among the evaluated methods while maintaining competitive attribution robustness and perplexity. Ablation studies show that reliable three-state detection requires a well-defined notion of intactness, co-embedding of the two signals, and complementary sensitivity to edits.

</details>


### [182] [PIPES: Securing Agent Perception with Provenance and Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12789)
*Sanjay Kariyappa,Severin Klingler,G. Edward Suh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PIPES（基于来源信息与先验强制的筛选）的防御机制，用于抵御工具使用智能体的状态腐化攻击。该机制利用语义先验和来源溯源信息筛选响应单元，将攻击成功率从84.7%降至2.3%，同时保持甚至略提升良性工具效用（92.5% vs 90.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用智能体会从不同可信度级别的外部来源消费数据，但工具响应通常不标明每个组件的生产者及其意图。这一缺口使得攻击者控制的内容能够超越其响应组件的信息权威性，对智能体感知的环境做出虚假声明，从而腐化智能体的状态，使后续攻击行为在现有防护机制看来是合理的。

Method: PIPES采用两层策略：1）当模式（schema）提供稳定预期时，利用静态字段契约进行筛选；2）对开放内容，根据预响应轨迹和可信来源元数据条件化筛选。系统将违反语义先验或来源层级的单元标记出来，部署时可选移除、警告、阻止或升级处理。评估中采用原子移除策略，并针对自适应PAIR风格攻击进行测试。

Result: 在VitaBench和AgentDyn各自的三个分割上，以Gemma 4 31B IT为目标智能体，PIPES将平均攻击成功率从84.7%降至2.3%，同时保留平均良性效用（PIPES为92.5%，无防御时为90.6%）。

Conclusion: PIPES通过结合来源溯源与语义先验的筛选机制，能够有效抵御状态腐化攻击，在不损失甚至提升良性性能的前提下大幅降低攻击成功率，为工具使用智能体的安全性提供了可行且有效的防御方案。

Abstract: Tool-using agents consume external data from sources with different levels of trust, yet tool responses rarely identify who produced each component or what it should convey. We show that this gap enables state-corruption attacks, in which attacker-controlled content makes environmental claims beyond the informational authority of its response component and corrupts the agent's perceived environment, making the resulting action appear justified to existing guardrails. We introduce PIPES (Provenance-Informed, Prior-Enforced Screening), which screens response units using semantic priors and source provenance. PIPES uses static field contracts when schemas provide stable expectations, and conditions screening of open-ended content on the pre-response trajectory and trusted provenance metadata. It marks units that violate their semantic prior or the provenance hierarchy; deployments may remove, warn, block, or escalate detected violations. We instantiate atomic removal and evaluate PIPES against adaptive PAIR-style attacks. Across the three VitaBench and three AgentDyn splits with Gemma 4 31B IT as the target agent, PIPES reduces average attack success from 84.7% to 2.3%, while preserving average benign utility (92.5% with PIPES versus 90.6% without defense).

</details>


### [183] [RealmEye: Virtual Machine Introspection for Arm CCA Realm VMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12822)
*Ruofei Qu,Wei Feng,Hongzhan Ma,Menghan Jia,Muyan Shen,Yu Qin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RealmEye 是首个面向 Arm CCA Realm VM 的虚拟机自省（VMI）系统，将自省逻辑完全置于 Realm Management Monitor（RMM）的 R-EL2 层级，实现了硬件强制的监控与受控 VM 分离，无需在 Realm 内运行代理，支持内存/寄存器读取、挂起快照、页级访问陷阱及周期性自驱动触发扫描，并通过硬件认证通道将结果返回远端所有者，且与 LibVMI、DRAKVUF 等现有工具兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密虚拟机（CVM）的硬件隔离在保护租户免受恶意云攻击的同时，也使得租户无法察觉VM内部运行的恶意程序（如内核rootkit），因为rootkit可利用网络或供应链攻击植入并隐藏在隔离保护之下。经典VMI依赖可信Hypervisor，而CVM将Hypervisor排除在TCB之外；现有AMD SEV-SNP的CVM-VMI方案（00SEVen）依赖VMPL0特权层的in-VM代理机制，但该机制在Arm CCA上不存在，导致Realm VM缺乏任何自省方案。

Method: RealmEye将完整的自省逻辑放置在Realm Management Monitor（RMM）的R-EL2特权层级中，利用硬件强制实现监控器与被监控Realm的分离——Realm内无代理且无需修改。它支持读取Realm内存和寄存器、挂起VM以获得一致快照、捕获页级访问，且完全依赖in-VM接口。提供周期性自驱动触发模式使扫描时序保持在RMM内部，防止Hypervisor与Realm内rootkit合谋。结果通过硬件认证通道返回远端所有者，并提供CCA驱动后端使LibVMI和DRAKVUF等现有工具无需修改即可互操作。

Result: 在Arm FVP平台上，RealmEye成功检测出Diamorphine rootkit的进程隐藏和syscall-table hooking行为。其in-RMM开销与原始调用次数呈线性可预测关系。

Conclusion: RealmEye填补了Arm CCA Realm VM缺乏虚拟机自省方案的空缺，通过将自省逻辑置于RMM中实现了无需in-VM代理的硬件级自省能力，为Realm VM提供了可靠的外部可见性，同时保持了与主流VMI工具生态的兼容性。

Abstract: Confidential VMs (CVMs) have become the dominant substrate for sensitive cloud workloads, from financial services to privacy-preserving AI inference. The hardware isolation that protects these CVMs from a malicious cloud also blinds their owners to what runs inside them: kernel rootkits planted via network or supply-chain attacks can hide processes, tamper with kernel data, and exfiltrate model weights under the cover of the same isolation that defends the VM. Tenants therefore need to inspect a running CVM from outside, yet classical VM introspection (VMI) presupposes a trusted Hypervisor, which CVMs exclude from the TCB. The state-of-the-art CVM-VMI system, 00SEVen, restores introspection on AMD SEV-SNP via an in-VM agent at a privileged tier (VMPL0), a mechanism that does not exist on Arm CCA, leaving Realm VMs without any introspection solution.
  We present RealmEye, the first VMI system for Arm CCA Realm VMs. RealmEye places the entire introspection logic inside the Realm Management Monitor (RMM) at R-EL2, achieving hardware-enforced separation between the monitor and the monitored VM: no agent runs inside the Realm, and the Realm remains unmodified. RealmEye reads Realm memory and registers, suspends the VM for consistent snapshots, and traps page-level accesses, without relying on any in-VM interface. A periodic, self-driven trigger mode keeps scan timing internal to the RMM, preventing the Hypervisor from colluding with in-Realm rootkits. Results are returned to the remote owner over a hardware-attested channel, and a CCA driver backend lets existing tools such as LibVMI and DRAKVUF interoperate with RealmEye unchanged. On the Arm FVP, RealmEye detects process hiding and syscall-table hooking by Diamorphine, and its in-RMM cost is linearly predictable from primitive invocation counts.

</details>


### [184] [Beyond Source: An Empirical Study of Python Bytecode Security Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12853)
*Baihong Chen,Tian Xie,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Python字节码（.pyc文件、编译模块及marshalled代码对象）作为安全工件的安全性问题，揭示了源代码审查与字节码实际执行之间的"检查-执行鸿沟"。通过对1,034,843个PyPI工件的大规模分析，发现7,388个包含字节码的工件（含228,578个.pyc文件），并评估了反编译工具的可分析性、CPython运行时在对抗性字节码下的鲁棒性以及字节码发现能否从源码复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python包安全审查主要基于源代码，但Python运行时可直接执行字节码（.pyc文件、仅编译模块、序列化代码对象），导致安全审查关注源代码而实际执行的是字节码，形成了"检查-执行鸿沟"（inspection-execution gap）。这一鸿沟可能被恶意行为者利用，使安全审查失效，因此需要实证研究字节码作为安全工件的特性。

Method: 研究包含四个维度：(1) 测量PyPI发行版中字节码的暴露程度（收集1,034,843个PyPI工件）；(2) 使用版本感知的反编译工具评估字节码的可分析性（覆盖CPython 3.8-3.14）；(3) 通过对抗性字节码（包括真实PyPI字节码和变异字节码）测试CPython运行时的鲁棒性，结合模糊测试生成运行时崩溃发现；(4) 测试源代码级能否复现字节码层面的分析发现。

Result: 识别出7,388个含字节码的工件、228,578个.pyc文件及28,193个工件本地无源码的.pyc文件。现代CPython字节码中204,904个在范围文件中至少有一个反编译器能产出源代码（204,901个），但仅为产出测量而非功能等价验证。工具鲁棒性差：真实PyPI字节码可触发托管代码异常和超时，变异字节码可导致反编译器原生进程崩溃，共产生17种鲁棒性特征。模糊测试产生1,009个堆栈去重后的运行时发现（以指针解引用症状为主），261组存在潜在内存损坏特征，至少91.7%的组到达了文档不安全的摄入边界之外。以上发现均无法从普通Python源码复现。

Conclusion: Python字节码是生态系统中可见的工件、实际可分析的安全目标，以及安全相关的解释器输入，其行为未必与源码级行为一致。这意味着纯源码导向的安全审查存在系统性盲区，安全工具链和审查流程需要考虑字节码层面的风险评估。

Abstract: Python package security is largely source-centric, yet Python runtimes can execute bytecode directly through .pyc files, compiled-only modules, and marshalled code objects, creating an inspection-execution gap. We present an empirical study of Python bytecode as a security artifact. We measure bytecode exposure in PyPI distributions, evaluate practical analyzability using version-aware tooling, assess CPython runtime robustness under adversarial bytecode, and test source-level reproduction of bytecode findings. Across 1,034,843 collected PyPI artifacts, we identify 7,388 bytecode-containing artifacts, including 228,578 .pyc files and 28,193 artifact-local source-less .pyc files. For modern CPython 3.8-3.14 bytecode, at least one selected decompiler emits source for 204,901 of 204,904 in-scope files, a result measuring emission rather than verified functional equivalence. Tools are non-robust: observed PyPI bytecode triggers managed-code exceptions and timeouts, while adversarial mutated bytecode also drives decompilers into native process failures; together these outcomes yield 17 distinct robustness signatures. Fuzzing produces 1,009 stack-deduplicated runtime findings dominated by pointer-dereference symptoms; 261 groups exhibit potential memory-corruption characteristics, and at least 91.7% of groups reach execution beyond the documented-unsafe ingestion boundary. None reproduce from ordinary Python source. Bytecode is thus a visible ecosystem artifact, a practical analysis target, and a security-relevant interpreter input whose behavior need not match source-level behavior.

</details>


### [185] [Discovering Persistent Behavioural Patterns for Interpretable Blockchain Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12864)
*Dorottya Zelenyanszki,Zhe Hou,Kamanashis Biswas,Vallipuram Muthukkumarasamy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个可扩展的、与应用无关的区块链行为模式发现框架，通过两级嵌入（句子级+序列级）从大规模区块链活动中挖掘持久行为模式，并在以太坊3000万交易上验证，能发现DEX交易、NFT活动、钓鱼、机器人操作、预言机操纵和跑路骗局等常规及恶意行为模式，且许多模式在独立观察窗口间保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链DeFi分析方法大多针对特定应用、难以扩展或难以解释，缺乏一个既能大规模处理又兼具可解释性的通用框架来发现持久的行为模式。

Method: 构建包含合约、代币和市场上下文的行为句子，然后采用两步嵌入流程：句子级嵌入捕获个体行为，序列级嵌入捕获用户随时间的行为序列；最后通过可解释的行为画像器，从行为主题、常规行为、时间动态、实体暴露和可疑性证据等维度刻画所发现的社区。

Result: 在以太坊超过3000万笔交易上评估，框架能够发现常规和恶意两类行为模式，涵盖DEX交易、NFT活动、钓鱼、机器人操作、预言机操纵和跑路骗局；许多发现的模式在独立观察窗口间保持稳定，支持跨时段长期行为识别。

Conclusion: 该框架兼具可扩展性、可解释性和持久性分析能力，可用于区块链取证调查、行为归因和威胁发现。

Abstract: Public blockchain data enables large-scale DeFi-related analysis, but many existing approaches are application-specific, difficult to scale, or hard to interpret. This research proposes a scalable, application-agnostic framework for \emph{persistent behavioural pattern discovery} from large-scale blockchain activity. It constructs behaviour sentences enriched with contract, token and market context, then applies a two-step embedding process: sentence-level embeddings capture individual actions, while sequence-level embeddings capture user behaviour over time. An interpretable behavioural profiler characterizes discovered communities through behavioural motifs, routines, temporal dynamics, entity exposure, and suspiciousness evidence. Evaluation on Ethereum using over 30 million transactions shows that the framework uncovers both routine and malicious behavioural patterns, including decentralised exchange (DEX) trading, NFT activity, phishing, bot operations, oracle manipulation, and rug-pull schemes. Importantly, many patterns remain stable across independent observation windows, enabling the identification of long-term behaviours beyond a single analysis period. The proposed framework combines scalability, interpretability, and persistence analysis, supporting blockchain forensic investigation, behavioural attribution, and threat discovery.

</details>


### [186] [Labels Are Not Endpoints: Treatment Leakage and Construct Validity in MCP Agent Security Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12880)
*Rana Muhammad Ahmed,Sabahat Abbas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对一次工具使用智能体的安全评估活动进行了审计，发现历史标注存在"处理泄露"（treatment leakage）问题——即标注类别被处理元数据所污染。通过处理盲重建，修正了58条错误标注，同时保留了三个受保护数据传输案例和一个独立转发案例。锁定版本v2中ATTACK_SUCCESS记录为零，而转发案例在语义边界处仍被标为HIJACK_ATTEMPT。论文贡献了七链完整链（Integrity Chain）和一个可执行的端点完整性检查器，但明确声明这是活动范围内的测量审计，而非总体攻击率、模型排名、防御效度或因果估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对工具使用智能体的安全评估往往将存储标签等同于行为事实，缺乏对标注过程和标注体系本身的审计。作者旨在揭示标签与真实行为之间的偏差，特别是标注系统内部的处理泄露问题，以纠正误导性的安全结论。

Method: 对保留的评估活动进行回溯审计：追踪10,200条执行记录到180个模型绑定请求、45个语义请求和15个可观察刺激。对比两个模式处理（schema treatments）的交付情况，识别出缺失的外部载荷族语料库。发现历史评分器存在直接处理泄露（处理元数据门控ATTACK_SUCCESS类别），随后进行处理盲重建，并采用双评审者盲法一致性审查来验证96个可结构化解释的请求。

Result: 处理盲重建纠正了58条历史ATTACK_SUCCESS或HIJACK_ATTEMPT标签为授权的良性完成，同时保留三条已验证的受保护数据转移案例和一条独立的未授权转发案例。锁定v2人口普查中ATTACK_SUCCESS记录为零，转发案例在语义边界处仍为HIJACK_ATTEMPT。双评审盲法共识与锁定v2在4个构造边界案例上存在分歧，但96个请求的评审者共识类别一致。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了七链完整链和可执行端点完整性检查器作为审计工具，强调这是一次活动范围内的测量审计。结论在于安全评估的标签可信度需要通过独立的、处理盲的复核来验证，且审计结果不应外推为总体攻击率、模型排名或防御效度的估计。

Abstract: Security evaluations of tool-using agents often equate stored labels with behavioral facts. We audit a preserved campaign by tracing 10,200 execution rows to 180 model-bound requests, 45 semantic requests, and 15 observable stimuli. Two schema treatments were delivered, but the planned external payload-family corpus was not. The historical grader exhibited direct treatment leakage: treatment metadata gated the ATTACK_SUCCESS class, so fixed behavior could change class under treatment relabeling. A treatment-blind reconstruction corrects 58 historical ATTACK_SUCCESS or HIJACK_ATTEMPT labels to authorized benign completions while preserving three verified protected-data transfers and one separate unauthorized-forwarding case. The locked v2 census contains exactly zero ATTACK_SUCCESS records, while the forwarding case remains a HIJACK_ATTEMPT at a semantic boundary concerning objective completion. A dual-reviewer blinded concordance review of all 96 requests deemed structurally interpretable by locked v2 produced identical reviewer-consensus classes but differed from the locked codebook on four construct-boundary cases. We contribute a seven-link Integrity Chain and an executable, scope-bounded endpoint-integrity linter. The result is a campaign-bounded measurement audit, not a population attack-rate, model-ranking, defense-efficacy, or causal estimate.

</details>


### [187] [Adversarial Robustness in Smishing Detection: A Comparative Analysis of Adversarial Fragility in Classical vs. Transformer-Based Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12889)
*Denzel Chiuseni,Athanase Bahizire,Silva Hama,Jema David Ndibwile*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究评估了五种模型架构（三种经典词法模型和两种多语言transformer）在短信钓鱼（smishing）检测中的对抗鲁棒性，使用27,037条消息数据集，跨越三种攻击类型和强度级别。结果显示经典模型在字符混淆和结构扰动下近乎灾难性失败（RDR高达0.988），而transformer表现出显著更强的韧性（RDR高达0.351），并验证了干净文本性能不能预测对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短信钓鱼检测系统通常在干净的单语文本上训练和评估，但攻击者常通过字符混淆、跨语言代码切换和结构扰动来规避这些系统，尤其在低资源场景下。现有研究缺乏对不同模型架构在对抗攻击下鲁棒性的系统比较。

Method: 使用27,037条消息数据集，对五种模型架构（Random Forest、XGBoost、CNN+BiLSTM、mBERT、XLM-RoBERTa）进行对抗鲁棒性评估。经典模型接受黑盒通用攻击，transformer采用注意力引导的目标攻击。每种模型测试三种攻击类型和强度级别，以鲁棒性退化比（RDR）为性能指标，辅以Cliff's d效应量、Mann-Whitney U和Friedman检验进行统计验证。

Result: 经典模型在字符混淆和结构扰动下RDR高达0.988，近乎灾难性失败；transformer最大RDR为0.351，表现出显著更强的韧性，结构扰动是其最明显的弱点。XLM-RoBERTa尽管干净文本基线更高，但退化程度高于mBERT。统计检验确认这些模式归因于模型架构而非采样。

Conclusion: 干净文本性能不能可靠预测对抗鲁棒性；不同架构间存在显著的鲁棒性分界。研究强调需要架构特定的防御策略，并将短信钓鱼检测重新定位为对抗性网络安全挑战，而非静态分类任务。

Abstract: Smishing detection systems are commonly trained and evaluated on clean, monolingual text. In low-resource settings, however, attackers frequently circumvent these systems through character obfuscation, cross-lingual code-switching, and structural perturbation. This study evaluates adversarial robustness for five model architectures: three classical lexical models (Random Forest, XGBoost, CNN+BiLSTM) and two multilingual transformers (mBERT, XLM-RoBERTa), using a dataset of 27,037 messages. Classical models are subjected to black-box generic attacks, while transformers are evaluated with attention-guided targeting. Each model is tested across three attack types and intensity levels, with performance measured by the Robustness Degradation Ratio (RDR). The results reveal a distinct architectural boundary: classical models experience near-catastrophic failure under character obfuscation and structural perturbation (RDR up to 0.988), whereas transformers demonstrate significantly greater resilience (RDR up to 0.351), with structural perturbation representing their most pronounced vulnerability. Effect-size analysis (Cliff's d) indicates a substantial difference between the two model categories. Within the transformer group, XLM-RoBERTa, despite achieving a higher clean-text baseline, exhibits greater degradation than mBERT. These findings demonstrate that clean-text performance is not a reliable predictor of adversarial robustness. Statistical validation using Mann-Whitney U and Friedman tests confirms that these patterns are attributable to model architecture rather than sampling. The results underscore the necessity for architecture-specific defences and frame smishing detection as an adversarial cybersecurity challenge rather than a static classification task.

</details>


### [188] [Beyond Handcrafted Security: Towards Self-Evolving Defense for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12977)
*Jiajun Ruan,Peiyang Li,Yukun Chen,Fengting Li,Chao Feng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"引擎框架"(harness)的运行时防御形式化框架，并在此基础上设计了HARD（基于引擎的自主运行时防御演化）系统，使大型语言模型代理的安全防御能够从手工工程转变为自动演化的过程，实验表明其在提升安全性能的同时保持了良性任务的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理的运行时防御方法严重依赖人工设计的干预措施，缺乏系统性的构建和维护框架，无法适应不断演化的安全威胁。作者旨在提供一个原理化的防御构建框架，并实现防御的自主演化，减少人工介入。

Method: 首先构建了引擎级(harness-level)的运行时防御形式化模型，系统描述引擎机制如何支持防御构建，统一了现有运行时防御干预的视角。在此形式化基础上提出了HARD框架，该框架能够自动识别合适的干预策略，并根据观察到的失败轨迹迭代改进防御工件，将防御开发从手动工程转化为自主演化过程。

Result: 大量实验表明，HARD在安全性能上优于现有手工设计的防御方案，同时保持良性任务的效用不受损害，验证了自主防御演化作为保障已部署LLM代理安全的新范式的有效性。

Conclusion: 自主防御演化是一个有前景的新范式，能使LLM代理主动识别自身防御弱点并持续改进保护机制，为已部署代理的安全保障提供了新的方向。

Abstract: The expanding operational capabilities of large language model (LLM) agents introduce sophisticated security threats. Runtime defenses have emerged as an effective approach to mitigating these risks by integrating security mechanisms into the agent execution loop. However, existing runtime defenses rely heavily on manually designed interventions and lack a principled framework for their construction and maintenance. In this work, we first develop a harness-level formulation of runtime defense that systematically characterizes how harness mechanisms enable defense construction and provides a unified view of existing runtime defense interventions from a harness perspective. Building on this formulation, we propose HARD (Harness-based Autonomous Runtime Defense Evolution), a self-evolving runtime defense framework that automatically identifies appropriate intervention strategies and iteratively improves defense artifacts based on observed failure traces. HARD transforms runtime defense development from manual engineering into an autonomous evolution process, and extensive experiments demonstrate that it improves security performance over existing handcrafted defenses while preserving benign task utility. Our findings highlight autonomous defense evolution as a promising new paradigm for securing deployed LLM agents, enabling agents to identify defense weaknesses and continuously improve their protection mechanisms.

</details>


### [189] [ATOBench: Tracing How Autonomous Penetration-Testing Agents Verify Vulnerabilities When Target Evidence Lies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12996)
*Qiyang Chen,Yixi Li,Fengwei Zhang,Junlin Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ATOBench，一个评估框架，通过注入注册的响应变换并配对本机回放，使自主渗透测试智能体的验证过程变得可观测，从而揭示不可信观测如何影响攻击轨迹、验证和报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主渗透测试智能体依赖目标响应指导后续动作和最终报告，欺骗性响应可以重定向攻击轨迹和验证过程，但最终报告无法揭示智能体如何解释冲突证据、改变路线、决定停止或转化观测为漏洞声明，因此需要让验证过程可观测。

Method: ATOBench在运行时注入注册的响应变换，并将每个变换后的episode与相同环境下的本机episode配对，在首个受影响响应处对齐。采用源链接重建方法跟踪后续动作、证据恢复、停止和报告支持。三个冻结的观测契约涵盖利用证明、资源所有权和可复用工件。评估五个模型路线共450个episode。

Result: 分析表明，增加的活动量可能掩盖断裂的验证链，而成功的恢复依赖于找到可用证据并在报告中保持其完整性。五个模型路线在450个episode上的评估验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: ATOBench将欺骗性目标观测转化为可复现的证据处理探针，通过过程级视角扩展了进攻性渗透测试智能体评估，超越了仅关注最终结果的局限，揭示了不可信观测如何塑造动作、验证和报告。

Abstract: Autonomous penetration-testing agents rely on target responses. These responses guide both subsequent actions and the final report. A deceptive response can therefore redirect both the attack trajectory and the agent's verification process. However, final reports reveal little about how an agent interprets conflicting evidence, changes course, decides to stop, or turns observations into a vulnerability claim. We introduce ATOBench, an evaluation framework that makes this verification process observable. ATOBench injects registered response transformations at runtime and pairs each transformed episode with a native episode under the same environment. Each pair is aligned at the first affected response. A source-linked reconstruction then follows later actions, evidence recovery, stopping, and report support. Three frozen observation contracts cover exploit proof, resource ownership, and reusable artifacts. We evaluate five model routes over 450 episodes. The analysis shows that increased activity can mask a broken verification chain, while successful recovery depends on finding usable evidence and preserving it through reporting. ATOBench turns deceptive target observations into a reproducible probe of evidence handling in autonomous penetration testing. This process-level view extends offensive pentest agent evaluation beyond final outcomes by revealing how untrusted observations shape actions, verification, and reporting.

</details>


### [190] [OmniSphinx: Active Mix Networks (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13008)
*Daniel Schadt,Christoph Coijanovic,Shabi Shabani,Thorsten Strufe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: OmniSphinx是一种新型混合网络数据包格式，发送方在数据包中嵌入代码以决定处理方式，使单个部署能够模拟任何其他混合网络格式，解决了现有格式互不兼容的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合网络的多种数据包格式互不兼容，各自针对单一特定目标设计，需要独立的软件和基础设施，导致部署和互操作成本高昂。

Method: 发送方在数据包中嵌入处理代码，创建主动式混合网络（active mix network），使得单个OmniSphinx部署能够模拟任何其他混合网络格式。

Result: 实验评估显示，与原生执行相比，OmniSphinx的模拟开销在典型混合网络用例中合理：对于最紧凑的Sphinx格式，计算时间增加约90微秒，头部大小增加33%。

Conclusion: OmniSphinx通过代码嵌入机制实现了多格式统一的混合网络部署，以可接受的性能开销换取格式兼容性和部署灵活性，有效解决了混合网络格式碎片化的问题。

Abstract: Mix networks are an important tool to implement anonymous communication, which protects not just the content but also the metadata of messages. Over time, various packet formats for mix networks have been proposed, usually with single, specific goals in mind. These formats are incompatible with each other, requiring separate software and infrastructure to be set up. In this paper, we propose a new format, OmniSphinx, which solves this issue. In OmniSphinx, senders embed code in their packets that determines how they must be processed. The resulting active mix network can emulate any other mix format within a single deployment. Our empirical evaluation shows that emulation in OmniSphinx incurs reasonable overhead compared to native execution for typical mix network use cases: For Sphinx, the most compact format, computation time increases by around 90μs, while headers increase by 33% in size.

</details>


### [191] [InterSAGE: The Secure and Verifiable Interoperability Protocol for An Internet of Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13030)
*Zhenhua Zou,Sheng Guo,Qiuyang Zhan,Lepeng Zhao,Shuo Li,Zhuotao Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: InterSAGE是一个面向LLM驱动智能体的信任原生协议套件，提供四层信任基础设施（持久身份、发现、信任协商、问责），旨在与MCP、A2A、ANP等通信协议互补共存，而不替代它们。其核心包括Agent身份卡、DID绑定的可验证凭证清单、能力衰减与两层级访问控制结合的信任协商，以及无需共识账本的内核中介加密审计追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物联网（Internet of Agents）协议主要定义智能体之间如何交换消息，但未解决智能体如何证明其身份、授权、声明能力以及委托后的可问责性问题。随着LLM智能体跨组织边界协作日益普及，这一安全信任基座缺口成为关键瓶颈。

Method: InterSAGE采用四层协议架构：（1）持久身份层，通过Agent身份卡绑定开发者、代码包、运营者及部署上下文；（2）发现层，使用DID绑定的可验证凭证清单实现能力感知发现；（3）信任协商层，结合单调能力衰减与两层级访问控制；（4）问责层，通过内核中介加密审计追踪，将使用、委托和执行痕迹绑定到智能体身份，且无需共识账本。该协议设计为与现有MCP、A2A、ANP、AG-UI互补，允许通信协议独立演进。

Result: 作者与超过50项相关工作进行了对比（涵盖智能体协议、去中心化身份、OAuth/OIDC扩展、零信任治理、委托和审计架构），结论是没有任何既有架构将持久身份、能力感知发现、信任协商和问责能力作为统一的四层信任基座联合实现，InterSAGE填补了这一空白。

Conclusion: InterSAGE提供了一个与通信协议正交的信任语义层，使得跨组织边界的智能体互操作能够显式、可移植且可验证地表达信任关系，同时不限制通信协议本身的演化，为安全智能体互操作建立了首个统一四层信任基座。

Abstract: The emerging Internet of Agents enables LLM-powered agents to discover peers, invoke tools, and delegate tasks across organizational boundaries. Existing protocols increasingly define how agents exchange messages, but not how an agent proves its identity, authorization, advertised capabilities, or accountability after delegation. We present InterSAGE, a trust-native protocol suite that supplies this missing security substrate alongside, rather than in place of, communication protocols. InterSAGE comprises four layers: Persistent Identity, Discovery, Trust Negotiation, and Accountability. Its four core primitives are: (1) Agent Identity Cards that bind developer, code package, operator, and deployment context; (2) capability-aware discovery using DID-bound Verifiable Credential manifests; (3) trust negotiation combining monotonic capability attenuation with two-tier access control; and (4) kernel-mediated cryptographic audit trails that bind usage, delegation, and execution traces to agent identity without a consensus ledger. InterSAGE is designed to complement MCP, A2A, ANP, and AG-UI, allowing communication protocols to evolve independently while keeping trust semantics explicit, portable, and verifiable. We compare InterSAGE with more than 50 efforts spanning agent protocols, decentralized identity, OAuth/OIDC extensions, zero-trust governance, delegation, and audit architectures. We show that no prior architecture jointly enforces persistent identity, capability-aware discovery, trust negotiation, and accountability as a unified four-layer trust substrate for secure agent interoperability.

</details>


### [192] [InSPECtor: Improving SLEIGH Processor Specification Veracity via Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13042)
*Michael Chesser,Paul Quirk,Douglas Cooke,Guy Farrelly,Surya Nepal,Damith C. Ranasinghe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了InSPECtor，一个首次系统化验证开源SLEIGH处理器规范（Ghidra使用）的测试框架。通过自动化代理pvalidation策略，从规范本身枚举可解码指令形式并生成目标初始状态，将使用该规范的模拟器与硬件参考进行差分测试。在x86-64、AArch64、ARM/Thumb、RISC-V、MSP430等多样化规范中发现38,920个差异，归约为125个带修复建议的独特bug，并提出8条改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理器规范支撑着反汇编器、反编译器、模拟器等关键安全和程序分析工具，但其正确性鲜少被检查。规范中的错误会扭曲程序行为、隐藏漏洞并助长分析规避技术，因此系统化验证处理器规范具有重要的安全意义，然而这一任务此前从未被系统化地实现过。

Method: 设计并实现了基于代理自动观测验证策略的测试框架。该方法利用规范本身编码的结构来枚举可解码的指令形式并生成有针对性的初始状态，然后通过将使用处理器规范的模拟器与硬件参考进行差分测试，验证指令的解码和模拟的正确性。

Result: 在覆盖x86-64、AArch64、ARM/Thumb、RISC-V、MSP430等多样化的开源规范中发现了超过38,920个差异，归因出125个带有修复建议的独特bug，涵盖了解码缺陷、语义缺陷以及跨厂商不一致性。同时提炼出8条具体建议以推动未来改进。

Conclusion: 该工作强调了规范正确性的重要性，提供了一个实用的工具以显著提升SLEIGH处理器规范的保真度，从而加强下游安全和分析工具的可靠性。

Abstract: Processor specifications underpin critical security and program- analysis tools such as disassemblers, decompilers, and emulators, yet, their correctness is rarely examined. Errors in specifications distort program behaviour, obscure vulnerabilities, and enable analysis-evasion techniques. Validating processor specifications is a non-trivial task. Our study is a significant undertaking to enable, for the first time, the systematic validation of open-source SLEIGH language specifications, predominantly used by Ghidra. We design and implement a testing framework based on an automated oracle validation strategy by proxy. Our approach leverages the structure encoded in a specification itself to enumerate decodable instruction forms and generate targeted initial states. Then differentially test the successful decoding and emulation of those instructions by comparing emulators exercising the processor specification against hardware references.
  Applying InSPECtor across diverse, open-source specifications---x86-64, AArch64, ARM/Thumb, RISC-V, MSP430---embedding differences in specification styles, author preferences, and instruction set architecture designs, we uncovered over 38,920 discrepancies that led to 125 unique bugs with proposed fixes, identifying decoding and semantic defects as well as cross-vendor inconsistencies. We distill our findings into 8 concrete recommendations to drive future improvements. Our work underscores the importance of specification correctness and provides a practical tool to substantially improve the fidelity of SLEIGH processor specifications, strengthening the reliability of downstream security and analysis tools.

</details>


### [193] [Operationalizing Cyber Threat Intelligence with GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13050)
*Atul Kabra,Prakhar Paliwal,Manjesh K. Hanawal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了将安全报告输入知识图谱检索系统（Microsoft GraphRAG）而非标准向量相似度检索系统（Naive RAG）时，能否生成更依赖金字塔顶部（持久、难以规避）线索的检测方案。案例研究显示，在旋转掉报告中所有IP、域名和文件哈希后，GraphRAG方案100%的检测仍有效，而Naive RAG方案仅有29%有效。在九个真实CTI报告上的重复实验证实了这一模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全研究人员发布网络攻击报告后，检测工程师需要将其转化为有效的检测规则。但大多数自动化尝试只提取简单的线索（IP地址、域名、文件哈希）并转为阻止列表，攻击者可在数小时或数天内更改这些线索，导致检测很快失效。这被称为"痛苦金字塔"（Pyramid of Pain）问题。本文旨在探索能否通过知识图谱检索生成依赖更持久、金字塔顶部线索的检测方案。

Method: 将同一安全报告分别输入Microsoft GraphRAG（知识图谱检索系统）和Naive RAG（标准向量相似度检索系统），两者使用相同的报告、相同的生成指令和相同的语言模型编写最终方案，仅检索步骤不同。通过详细的APT28报告案例研究（旋转所有IP、域名和文件哈希后检测存活率），并在来自四个供应商的九个真实CTI报告上进行重复对比实验。

Result: 在APT28案例研究中，GraphRAG方案在旋转所有简单指标后仍保持100%检测有效性，而Naive RAG方案仅保持29%。在九个CTI报告上的重复实验证实了相同模式：GraphRAG方案始终达到金字塔更高、更难规避的层次，即使两个系统在总分上接近时也是如此。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持将知识图谱感知检索视为自动生成SOC可部署狩猎方案的正确架构基础，同时表明生成提示词的措辞与检索后端本身几乎同样重要。

Abstract: When a security researcher publishes a report on a cyberattack, detection engineers are supposed to turn it into working detection rules. In practice, most automated attempts at this only extract the simplest clues from the report --- bad IP addresses, domain names, and file hashes --- and turn them into block lists. This is a weak strategy, because attackers can change these simple clues within hours or days, so the resulting detections stop working almost as soon as they are deployed. Security teams describe this idea with the Pyramid of Pain. This project asks whether feeding a report into a knowledge-graph retrieval system, Microsoft GraphRAG, rather than a standard vector-similarity retrieval system (Naive RAG), produces detection plans that rely more on these durable, top-of-pyramid clues. Both systems are given the same report, the same generation instructions, and the same language model to write the final plan; only the retrieval step differs. In a detailed case study of one APT28 report, the GraphRAG plan kept firing at 100\% of its detections after every IP address, domain, and file hash in the report was rotated, while the Naive RAG plan kept firing at only 29\%. Repeating the comparison across nine real CTI reports from four vendors confirms the same pattern: GraphRAG plans consistently reach higher, harder-to-evade levels of the pyramid, even when the two systems end up close on total score. The results support treating knowledge-graph-aware retrieval as the architecturally correct foundation for automatically generating SOC-deployable hunting plans, while showing that the wording of the generation prompt matters almost as much as the retrieval back-end itself.

</details>


### [194] [A Commitment-Based Hybrid Post-Quantum Cryptographic Model for Multi-File Cloud Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13138)
*Lemdi Frank Prikutse,Regina Esi Turkson,Alimatu-Saadia Yussiff,Abdul-Lateef Yussiff,Maame G. Asante-Mensah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于承诺的混合后量子认证模型，通过SHA3-256批量承诺将批量文件上传中的后量子签名次数从n次降为1次，消除了每文件格签名的性能瓶颈。在n=1000时，签名阶段时间相比逐文件双签名基线分别减少629倍（100 KB）、606倍（1 MB）和725倍（10 MB）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云存储客户端需要抵御未来量子攻击的认证方案，通常采用经典原语与标准化后量子算法相结合的混合构造。然而，将这种构造朴素地扩展到多文件上传场景时，每文件都需要一次格基签名计算，签名开销主导认证时间，在真实批处理规模下变得不可接受。

Method: 提出基于承诺的混合后量子认证模型，包含三个层次：(1) AES-256-GCM批量加密；(2) X25519与ML-KEM-768混合密钥封装；(3) Ed25519与ML-DSA-65双签名，对SHA3-256批量承诺进行计算。该承诺将所有密文绑定为单个固定大小的摘要，仅需一次签名，将每批的后量子签名调用次数从n次减少到1次（与批大小无关），其余加密和哈希操作由快速对称吞吐量支撑。

Result: 在商品化客户端平台上，20次重复实验取平均表明：批大小增长时签名阶段时间近似恒定，而逐文件基线呈线性增长。在n=1000时，对于100 KB、1 MB和10 MB文件，签名阶段时间分别减少629倍、606倍和725倍（对比共享其他所有原语的逐文件双签名基线）。

Conclusion: 该模型通过批量承诺机制将后量子签名计算从每文件一次优化为每批一次，在保持混合后量子安全性的同时，使签名阶段开销与批大小无关，显著提升了大规模云存储批量上传的认证效率。

Abstract: Cloud storage clients increasingly require authentication that remains secure against future quantum-capable adversaries, motivating hybrid constructions that combine classical primitives with standardized post-quantum alternatives. Extended naively to multi-file upload, such constructions incur a per-file lattice signing cost that dominates authentication time and becomes prohibitive at realistic batch sizes. This paper presents a commitment-based hybrid post-quantum model that addresses this bottleneck. It comprises AES-256-GCM bulk encryption, a hybrid X25519 with ML-KEM-768 key encapsulation mechanism, and a hybrid Ed25519 with ML-DSA-65 dual signature, computed over a SHA3-256 batch commitment. The commitment binds all ciphertexts in a batch to a single fixed-size digest that is signed once, reducing the number of post-quantum signature invocations per batch from n to one, independent of batch size; the remaining encryption and hashing is bounded by fast symmetric throughput. On a commodity client platform, averaged over 20 repetitions, this holds signing-phase time near-constant as the batch grows while the per-file baseline scales linearly. At n = 1000, the model reduces signing-phase time by factors of 629, 606, and 725 for 100 KB, 1 MB, and 10 MB files respectively, against a per-file dual-signing baseline sharing every other primitive.

</details>


### [195] [Smart Contract Invariants Protect Against Cybercriminals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13191)
*Sofia Bobadilla,Humaira Afrin,Angela Novelli,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文主张程序不变量（program invariants）是应对区块链智能合约安全攻击最有力的解决方案。作者构建了INVARIANTEVAL基准（包含28个真实以太坊漏洞案例及对应的人为编写的不变量），并通过PONDEREPLAY回放框架验证这些不变量能够阻止所有攻击——共回放了108,637条历史交易，实验证明智能合约不变量可有效防御网络罪犯的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链是最具对抗性的计算环境之一，大量资金被利用漏洞的罪犯窃取，但迄今没有概念或技术被证明能真正改变这一状况。作者主张经典的程序不变量概念可能是解决该问题的最有力方案。

Method: 设计了一个原创的实验协议：(1) 研究不变量能否防御过去的真实攻击；(2) 检验最先进的自动化工具是否能发现这些不变量。工具链基于INVARIANTEVAL基准（28个真实以太坊漏洞，每个配有人类编写的不变量），并用PONDEREPLAY回放框架重新执行交易以证明智能合约不变量的正确性和可靠性。

Result: 不变量成功阻止了INVARIANTEVAL中所有网络犯罪攻击，并通过对108,637条历史交易的回放进行了完整验证。大规模实验清楚表明智能合约不变量能有效防御网络罪犯。

Conclusion: 智能合约不变量是防御区块链网络攻击的有效且可靠的手段，经过大规模历史交易回放验证，该方案在真实场景中具有实际防护能力。

Abstract: Blockchains are among the most adversarial environments in computing. Billions are stolen by cybercriminals who exploit vulnerabilities.
